# 50.1-SEED-PROBE: operator live seed probe for the current model range (Phase 50.1, SURFACE-06, D-14 and D-18)

This file is the sole seed source for plan 50.1-08 (and 50.1-09), which add the new seeded rows; anything that is not in
it must be left out. It extends, and does not replace, `50-SEED-PROBE.md` (the 2026-10-15 transcript the existing rows
came from). The header below is Claude's curation of the transcript; the transcript follows the `---` rule verbatim.

## Provenance

- Who ran it (a deviation from the plan, recorded truthfully): Claude, as the execute-phase orchestrator, ran the
  harness at the operator's (Am0rfu5) explicit direction, inside the operator's own devcontainer, on the operator's own
  vendor keys. Plan 50.1-07 assumed that no vendor key exists in this environment and that the operator would run the
  probe on a separate machine (a `checkpoint:human-action`). That premise was stale: all seven vendor keys resolve in
  this devcontainer, bind-mounted read-only from the operator's host `~/.config/paladin/`. The operator chose "run it
  here". The operator did not themselves execute the harness; the operator did review and approve the
  operator-evidence section described below.
- Two runs are concatenated in the transcript, in this order. Each has its own `# Live vendor probe` heading with a
  UTC date line and a `commit:` line, quoted here from the transcript:
  - Run 1: `date (UTC): 2026-10-20 15:20:29Z`, `commit: 8ad298dd`. All seven credential variables were set
    (`OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, DEEPSEEK_API_KEY, MOONSHOT_API_KEY, DASHSCOPE_API_KEY, XAI_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY`
    as printed on its credential-variables line, names only). The candidate set was the 58 final ids. Exit status 0 and
    empty stderr, as reported by the runner.
  - Run 2: `date (UTC): 2026-10-20 15:55:58Z`, `commit: 8ad298dd`. Only `OPENAI_API_KEY, DASHSCOPE_API_KEY, XAI_API_KEY`
    were visible; the other four credential variables were deliberately hidden for this run, so its Anthropic,
    DeepSeek, Kimi and Gemini sections read SKIPPED by design (the printed reason says "not set"), not because those
    keys are absent. Purpose: retry the five candidates that had lost exactly one probe row in run 1 to a client-side
    transport failure (`UNREACHABLE: error sending request`): `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-5`, `grok-4.7`, `qwen3.7-flash`, `qwen3.5-flash`. The operator
    approved the retry. Exit status 0 and empty stderr, as reported by the runner. Run 2 rows supersede run 1 rows
    only for those five ids, and in practice only for the four that run 2 measured cleanly (see "Candidate set").
- Both runs ran at repository commit `8ad298dd`. The harness is Phase 50's `live_vendor_smoke --probe`.
- The raw transcript was never committed or pushed: it stayed an untracked, git-excluded local file until this scrub,
  which is stricter than the plan's "commit the raw file" path. It is deleted in the same commit that adds this file.
- Curated on 2026-10-20. The transcript that follows the `---` rule is verbatim, including its
  `## Operator-supplied evidence` section. No measured value was edited. The only redaction-relevant text the harness
  emits is a credential header name (the word `Authorization`, `x-api-key` or `x-goog-api-key` printed as a name, with
  its value never printed) and the credential variable names; both are names, not values.
- Operator-supplied evidence: the section at the end of the transcript holds vendor-documentation values (a research
  agent read only the vendors' own docs pages on 2026-10-20, each with its URL, access date and verbatim quote). They are
  DOCS, never observed by the probe, and are labelled as such inside the section. The operator reviewed and approved the
  section as appended. Where the vendor's own pages conflict (the `qwen3.7-plus` and `qwen3.7-flash` maximum output:
  131072 on the Model Info pages, "64k" on the visual-understanding page) the operator resolved it to the Model Info
  value; both quotes are recorded in the section.
- Scrub check (plan 50.1-07 Task 3): the raw file and the assembled file were each scanned, with a scratch script, for
  everything the Rust `redact_secret_patterns` function recognises (the HTTP authorization-scheme token marker in both
  capitalisations, the vendor-prefixed secret-key marker, the AWS access-key-id marker, the key and token query-value
  markers at a word boundary, and three-segment base64url triples), plus the Google, xAI, GitHub and Slack key shapes,
  long mixed-case tokens, authorization-style header lines that carry a value, and the literal value of every credential
  variable present in this environment and of every file under the operator's `~/.config/paladin/` (values compared
  programmatically, never printed). The scanner was first shown to fire on a planted sample. Nothing matched, so nothing
  was redacted or edited. The scratch script and its results are not part of this file.
- The transcript part of this file is byte-identical to the local raw file: a `diff` of the two, run immediately before
  the raw file was deleted, was empty.

## Providers PROBED and SKIPPED

Run 1 PROBED (7 of 9): OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, Kimi, Qwen, Grok, Gemini. Run 1 SKIPPED (2 of 9), as in
`50-SEED-PROBE.md`: Ollama (`SKIPPED (OLLAMA_BASE_URL not set)`: no local daemon is configured) and OpenAI-compatible
(`SKIPPED (OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_API_KEY not set)`: no endpoint is configured).

Run 2 PROBED (3 of 9): OpenAI, Qwen, Grok. Run 2 SKIPPED (6 of 9): Anthropic, DeepSeek, Kimi and Gemini (SKIPPED by
design: their credential variables were hidden for the retry; the printed reason is "not set"), and Ollama and
OpenAI-compatible for the same reasons as in run 1.

The Kimi curated default list contains the two code-specialised ids, which is why they are probed although the owner
struck them from the candidates. The Qwen curated default list contains `qwen3-max` for the same reason. In the Grok
listing the two candidate ids `grok-4.20-reasoning` and `grok-4.20-non-reasoning` do not appear as listing rows: they
are aliases of the listing rows `grok-4.20-0309-reasoning` and `grok-4.20-0309-non-reasoning` (the listing prints each
row's `aliases=` field). Every other candidate id appears as a row of its provider's live listing.

Quota-shaped errors: the only quota-shaped body in either run is the DashScope rate-limit body on `qwen-turbo`, a
curated default and not a candidate, in both runs: `You have exceeded your current request limit.`, body type `limit_requests` (as in
`50-SEED-PROBE.md`). No candidate hit a 429. The Gemini key is now paid tier (the operator's statement); no Gemini quota
error appears in run 1.

### Candidate set

Drafted: 61 ids, in `50.1-PROBE-CANDIDATES.md` (the planner's D-15 draft at branch head `bb1676de`). Struck by the owner
before the run: `kimi-k2.7-code`, `kimi-k2.7-code-highspeed` and `qwen3-max` (the three that table marked RECOMMEND
STRIKE). Added: none. Final set: 58 ids (the delta is 61 - 3 + 0 = 58).

| vendor | drafted | struck | added | final |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Anthropic | 10 | 0 | 0 | 10 |
| DeepSeek | 1 | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| OpenAI | 21 | 0 | 0 | 21 |
| Gemini | 12 | 0 | 0 | 12 |
| Grok | 3 | 0 | 0 | 3 |
| Kimi | 2 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| Qwen | 12 | 1 | 0 | 11 |
| total | 61 | 3 | 0 | 58 |

The three struck ids still appear in the transcript, because the harness always probes each provider's curated default
list and they are on it. They are NOT candidates and must not be offered for seeding. What the transcript shows for
them, for the record: `kimi-k2.7-code` and `kimi-k2.7-code-highspeed` answered the default call with
`invalid thinking: only type=enabled is allowed for this model`; `qwen3-max` answered the default call with `Model not exist.` (404).

Measured end to end means: the default call was ACCEPTED, and every other probe row of that model in the source run was
ACCEPTED, NOT APPLICABLE, or a parameter-level 4xx rejection of that one parameter value; no row was UNREACHABLE, a 429
or a 5xx. This is Claude's reading of the transcript and decides no seed value. Result for the 58 final candidates:
50 measured end to end (Anthropic 10, DeepSeek 1, OpenAI 17, Gemini 9, Qwen 11, Grok 2);
7 whose default call the vendor rejected; 1 not measured end to end (`grok-4.7`). Four of the 50 are taken from run 2
(`gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-5`, `qwen3.7-flash`, `qwen3.5-flash`): each lost one row to `UNREACHABLE: error sending request` in run 1 and had a clean
run 2. For those four the run 1 table is superseded by the run 2 table for the row that failed; the other rows of the

### Candidates not measured end to end (must not be seeded)

Default call rejected by the vendor (7). These are recorded, not seedable:

- `gpt-5.5-pro`, `gpt-5.4-pro`, `gpt-5.2-pro`: run 1, default parameters, `REJECTED (404)`: "This is not a chat model and thus not supported in the v1/chat/completions endpoint. Did you mean to use v1/completions?"
- `gpt-5-pro`: run 1, default parameters, `REJECTED (404)`: "This model is only supported in v1/responses and not in v1/chat/completions."
- `gemini-2.5-pro`, `gemini-2.5-flash`, `gemini-2.5-flash-lite`: run 1, default parameters, `REJECTED (404)`: each says
  "... is no longer available to new users." and names a newer model to use instead.

Not measured end to end (1):

- `grok-4.7`: the `temperature=2.00 (declared high end)` row is `UNREACHABLE: error sending request` in BOTH runs, so the temperature upper
  bound was never measured for it. Its other twelve rows are ACCEPTED or parameter-level 4xx rejections. The same row
  also failed for three Grok curated defaults: `grok-4.3` (UNREACHABLE in both runs), `grok-4.5` (REJECTED (500):
  "Internal error during token parsing" in run 1, UNREACHABLE in run 2), and `grok-4.6` (ACCEPTED (200) in run 1, UNREACHABLE in run 2). That
  points at an xAI-side or transport-side failure at `temperature=2.00` that is reproducible for several models, but the
  transcript does not say why, and this header does not guess.

### The two SURFACE-06 models

Both were probed in run 1 and produced a usable outcome on every probe row, so both are measured end to end. This
header decides no value.

- `claude-opus-4-8` (Anthropic, run 1, table `### Probes: `claude-opus-4-8``, 13 rows): ACCEPTED (200) on default
  parameters, `temperature=1.00`, one stop sequence, five stop sequences and `max_tokens=64` (5 rows); REJECTED (400) on
  `temperature=0.00` and `temperature=0.50` ("`temperature` is deprecated for this model."), on `temperature=1.01` ("temperature: range: 0..1") and on `top_p=0.5`
  ("`top_p` is deprecated for this model.") (4 rows); NOT APPLICABLE on the two penalties ("this wire has no such request field") and on both JSON
  modes ("Anthropic has no JSON mode") (4 rows). No UNREACHABLE, 429 or 5xx row. Its listing row reads `max_input_tokens=1000000 ; max_tokens=128000` and
  `lifecycle=active`.
- `deepseek-v4-pro` (DeepSeek, run 1, table `### Probes: `deepseek-v4-pro``, 13 rows): ACCEPTED (200) on default
  parameters, `temperature=0.00`, `temperature=2.00`, `temperature=0.50`, `top_p=0.5`, both penalties, one and five stop
  sequences, `max_tokens=64` and json_object mode (11 rows); REJECTED (400) on `temperature=2.01` ("Invalid temperature value, the valid range of temperature is [0, 2]") and on
  json_schema mode ("This response_format type is unavailable now") (2 rows). No UNREACHABLE, 429 or 5xx row.

### Re-measured already-seeded models

The harness probes each provider's curated default models, which are the models already seeded on 2026-10-15
(`crates/paladin-llm/src/registry/seeded.rs`): `claude-haiku-5-5`, `gpt-5.4-nano`, `deepseek-flash`, `kimi-k2.6`,
`kimi-k3`, `qwen3.8-flash`, `qwen-plus`, `grok-4.3`, `grok-4.6`, `grok-4.5`, `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`,
`gemini-3.6-flash`, `gemini-3.5-flash` (`gpt-4o-mini` is seeded but is not a curated default and was not probed).
Each was compared probe row by probe row, by probe name, against its table in the 2026-10-15 transcript
(`50-SEED-PROBE.md`). D-18: those rows are NOT edited by this plan; plan 50.1-08 flags the differences. Listing
fields were compared too.

Same outcome on every comparable row (only request ids, usage-field order and similar run noise differ): `gpt-5.4-nano`
(both runs), `claude-haiku-5-5`, `deepseek-flash`, `qwen3.8-flash` (both runs), `qwen-plus` (both runs),
`grok-4.3` (both runs), `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`. Listing fields are identical for all of them.

Differences found, each with both values:

| model | probe | 2026-10-15 (`50-SEED-PROBE.md`) | 2026-10-20 (this file) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `grok-4.6` | `temperature=2.00 (declared high end)` | UNREACHABLE: error sending request | run 1: ACCEPTED (200); run 2: UNREACHABLE: error sending request |
| `grok-4.5` | `temperature=2.00 (declared high end)` | UNREACHABLE: error sending request | run 1: REJECTED (500) "Internal error during token parsing"; run 2: UNREACHABLE: error sending request |
| `gemini-3.6-flash` | `max_tokens=64` | ACCEPTED (200), finish STOP, content non-empty | ACCEPTED (200), finish MAX_TOKENS, content EMPTY |
| `gemini-3.5-flash` | `max_tokens=64` | ACCEPTED (200), finish MAX_TOKENS, content non-empty | ACCEPTED (200), finish MAX_TOKENS, content EMPTY |
| `kimi-k2.6`, `kimi-k3` | probe set | 13 rows; the harness then declared a Kimi temperature range of `1..1` | 14 rows; the harness now declares `0..1` and so adds a `temperature=0.00 (declared low end)` row, which both models REJECTED (400) "invalid temperature: only 0.6 is allowed for this model"; every row that existed before has the same outcome |

Other, smaller differences: the usage-field set differs on the `json_object` and `json_schema` rows of `kimi-k2.6` and on
the `top_p=0.5` row of `gemini-3.6-flash` (outcome unchanged); the Kimi listing rows differ only in their `created=`
timestamp (`context_length` unchanged: 262144 for `kimi-k2.6`, 1048576 for `kimi-k3`). The 2026-10-15 `grok-4.3` row
and all other limit fields are unchanged.

None of these changes an ACCEPTED/REJECTED outcome that a seeded value rests on except the two Grok temperature
upper-bound rows, whose seeded comment says the `2.00` probe was UNREACHABLE and rests on the vendor's own
"less than 2" rejection text; `grok-4.6` now has one ACCEPTED `2.00` observation and one UNREACHABLE one, and
`grok-4.5` has none. Plan 50.1-08 decides what to do about them.

## DeepSeek

- base URL: `https://api.deepseek.com/v1`
- chat endpoint: `POST https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions`; credential header `Authorization` (name only, value never printed)
- models endpoint: `GET https://api.deepseek.com/v1/models`
- declared temperature range: 0..2
- every call carries `max_tokens` = 256 and a 120 s timeout; redirects are never followed

- every call carries `max_tokens` = 256 and a 120 s timeout; redirects are never followed

## Gemini

- base URL: `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta`
- chat endpoint: `POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-3.5-flash-lite:generateContent`; credential header `x-goog-api-key` (name only, value never printed)
- models endpoint: `GET https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?pageSize=1000`
- declared temperature range: 0..2
- every call carries `generationConfig.maxOutputTokens` = 256 and a 120 s timeout; redirects are never followed

- base URL: `https://api.x.ai/v1`
- chat endpoint: `POST https://api.x.ai/v1/chat/completions`; credential header `Authorization` (name only, value never printed)
- models endpoint: `GET https://api.x.ai/v1/models`
- declared temperature range: 0..2
- every call carries `max_tokens` = 256 and a 120 s timeout; redirects are never followed

## Operator-supplied evidence

**Every value in this section is DOCS, not observed.** None was measured by the probe above. Each value was read from the vendor's own public documentation on 2026-10-20 (the access date for every row). Claude looked the values up at the operator's direction, and the operator reviewed them before this transcript was committed. No vendor API was called for this section.

Scope: only candidates measured end to end by one of the two probe runs above (every probe row produced a usable outcome) and whose live listing does not expose the value. That means the context window and maximum output of the OpenAI and Qwen ids, and the maximum output of the Grok ids. The section also covers (c): the documented stop-sequence maximum for each such model that rejected five stop sequences. Anthropic, DeepSeek and Gemini listings expose their limits, so none of those ids needs a docs value. A value the docs do not state is written `not documented`, and that field stays unset.

### (b) Grok (xAI): maximum output (2 ids measured end to end)

The context windows come from the live listing above. Only the maximum output is sought here.

| id | max output | source URL | accessed | verbatim quote | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| grok-4.20-reasoning | not documented | https://docs.x.ai/developers/models/grok-4.20-reasoning | 2026-10-20 | (no maximum output stated on the page, its `.md` mirror, or https://docs.x.ai/llms-full.txt) | alias of `grok-4.20-0309-reasoning`; field stays unset |
| grok-4.20-non-reasoning | not documented | https://docs.x.ai/developers/models/grok-4.20-non-reasoning | 2026-10-20 | (no maximum output stated anywhere in the docs) | alias of `grok-4.20-0309-non-reasoning`; field stays unset |

The 128,000 in xAI's API reference is not a maximum. It is the request default when no limit is set ("Defaults to 128,000 when unset; set a larger value to allow longer generations.").

### (c) Documented stop-sequence maximum for measured models that rejected five stop sequences

The models are every OpenAI id in the (b) table above, plus `grok-4.20-reasoning`.

| vendor API | applies to | documented max stop sequences | source URL | accessed | verbatim quote |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| OpenAI Chat Completions (`stop`) | the 17 OpenAI ids above | 4 | https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/chat/subresources/completions/methods/create | 2026-10-20 | "Up to 4 sequences where the API will stop generating further tokens. The returned text will not contain the stop sequence." The same entry adds: "Not supported with latest reasoning models o3 and o4-mini." |
| xAI Chat Completions (`stop`) | grok-4.20-reasoning | 4 | https://docs.x.ai/developers/rest-api-reference/inference/chat-completions | 2026-10-20 | "(Not supported by reasoning models) Up to 4 sequences where the API will stop generating further tokens." |
